# CLAUDE.md

This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.

## Commands

```bash
npm run dev       # Start Next.js dev server
npm run build     # Production build
npm run lint      # ESLint (next/core-web-vitals)
npm start         # Start production server
npm run typecheck # tsc --noEmit
npm test          # node:test suite, tests/*.test.cjs (uses tsx to load TS)
```

`.github/workflows/checks.yml` runs typecheck, lint, `npm test`, `scripts/build-training-parts.ts --check` and `scripts/review-question-banks.py` on every PR and push to main; Vercel's preview covers the build. Run `npm test` before pushing, and when a test asserts on source text that a deliberate change moved, update the test to the new contract rather than deleting it.

## Architecture

**Tigertest** (always written "Tigertest" in copy and the logo; never TigerTest or tigertest, except in URLs, keys and the `TigerTestApp` user-agent token) is a DMV practice test app built with Next.js 15 (App Router), React 19, TypeScript, and Firebase.

### Stack
- **UI:** Tailwind CSS + shadcn/ui (Radix primitives) in `/components/ui/`
- **State:** Zustand store (`/store/useStore.ts`) with localStorage persistence + Firebase Firestore sync
- **Auth:** Firebase Authentication (email/password + Google OAuth) via `/contexts/AuthContext.tsx`
- **Backend:** Firebase Firestore for user data, Resend for transactional email
- **Path alias:** `@/*` maps to project root

### Key Data Flow

All question data lives in `/data/questions.json` (~2,200 questions covering 50 states + DC). Questions are either `Universal` (shared across states) or `State-Specific`.

**Test generation** (`/lib/testGenerator.ts`) is deterministic — each of 4 tests gets a fixed slice of questions:
- Test N gets universal questions `[(N-1)*40 .. N*40-1]` + state-specific `[(N-1)*10 .. N*10-1]`
- 50 questions per test, 200 total per state
- Answer options are shuffled every time a question is shown, in practice tests, training sets (first showing included, since Nov 2026: the banks are written with keys rotating A, B, C, D) and quizzes; skipped for position-dependent answers like "A and B" (`lib/shuffleOptions.ts`, pinned over every bank by `tests/shuffle-options.test.cjs`)

**Training sets** mirror tests: 4 sets of 50 questions with mastery-based progression and a wrong-answer queue for spaced repetition.

### Zustand Store Structure

The store in `/store/useStore.ts` is the single source of truth. Key sections:
- `selectedState` — drives which questions are loaded; clearing state clears all progress
- `currentTests` — in-progress test sessions (questions, answers, timestamps)
- `completedTests` — full history of finished tests
- `training` / `trainingSets` — training mode progress with mastery tracking
- Firebase sync: `loadUserData()`, `saveToFirestore()`, `convertGuestToUser()`
- Data versioning (`DATA_VERSION = 2`) with migration logic on load
- **Finished tests are stored compactly** (`lib/testHistoryCompact.ts`): in memory every `completedTests` session holds its full questions, but in `users/{uid}` an attempt whose questions can be rebuilt from a newer whole attempt of the same test stores each question as a pointer (`{questionId, p}`, plus `qref`, the id of that whole attempt) and is rebuilt on load by `expandCompletedTests()`. A question is only stored as a pointer when rebuilding it gives back exactly the same object, checked when it is written, so nothing is lost; an attempt in another language or from before a bank edit stays whole and older ones like it point at it. It keeps the doc away from Firestore's 1 MiB limit, past which every save fails (whole, a finished test is about 27 KB; as pointers about 7 KB). **It does not help an account that takes many different tests once each** (40 different tests is still about 1.1 MB), and `trainingAnswerHistory` still grows about 85 bytes an answer: the real fix for both is moving history out of the user doc. Server code that reads questions from anything but a test's latest attempt must call `expandCompletedTests()` first (`getRecentTestSession` does); `answers` are never changed; never filter or rewrite stored sessions one by one on the server (a pointer and the attempt it names must stay or go together). `scripts/user-doc-sizes.ts` lists the accounts closest to the limit. Tests: `tests/test-history-*.test.cjs` (a seeded fuzz over every bank, the Firestore client's own validation) and `tests/store-history-roundtrip.test.cjs` (the real store against an in-memory Firestore).
- **Saves are queued** (`lib/saveQueue.ts`): `saveToFirestore()` takes a numbered snapshot and runs one whole-doc transaction at a time, folding calls made meanwhile into one save of the newest state. A write that has not settled in 20 seconds stops holding the queue, and a save still waiting when the page is hidden or left is started at once; because two can then be in flight, the transaction itself skips a snapshot older than one already saved from this page. `flushPendingSaves()` is awaited on sign-out. Three failed saves in a row show `components/SyncNotice.tsx` (root layout). Still true and still a risk: `loadUserData` replaces this device's copy with the account's, so progress that never reached the account is gone on the next load.

### Routing

App Router pages in `/app/`. Key routes:
- `/` — Tigertest hub homepage. Since Nov 2026 every English homepage (`/` and the English country homepages) opens on the search (`components/home/SearchHome.tsx`, described under `/home-v2` below, which now redirects to `/`) with the questions answered this week under the tagline, then Popular this week, the DMV state grid (US) or the country's own tests, a compact "every test" list of plain links (`catalogLinksFor()` in `HomePage.tsx`: every catalog group on `/`, the country's own and worldwide tests elsewhere; it is the homepage's crawlable path to each landing, so keep it), the FAQ and Request a test; How it works and the Reddit quotes went, and the full card catalog lives at `/tests`. The search page's `h1` is the wordmark plus a screen-reader-only ": free practice tests for N exams". A homepage in another language (`/de`, a picked language via `app/home-lang`) keeps the old layout below. Old layout: "Popular this week" (six tests ranked by questions answered in the last 7 days from `analytics/questions.byExam`, `lib/server/popularTests.ts`, revalidated hourly), a DMV state grid, how it works with real app screenshots, the full catalog and a Tigertest FAQ. Catalog cards show a flag per language from `TestCatalogEntry.languages` (the DMV's es/vi/ko/zh, a v1 exam's `translations`)
- **Country (region)** (`lib/region.ts`, Oct 2026): every country has its own indexable homepage, the US at `/` and the others at `/uk`, `/ie`, `/ca`, `/au`, `/nz`, `/in`, `/ph`, `/my`, `/sg`, `/hk`, `/ae`, `/za`, `/ng`, `/ke`, `/pk`, `/es`, `/pt`, `/ro`, `/se`, `/gr`, `/dk`, `/hu`, `/be`, `/fi` and `/mt` (`app/{uk,ie,ca,au,nz,in,ph,my,sg,hk,ae,za,ng,ke,pk,es,pt,ro,se,gr,dk,hu,be,fi,mt}/page.tsx`; India, region `IN`, added Oct 30 2026 with the RTO learner's licence test, its first exam; the Philippines, Malaysia, Singapore, Hong Kong, UAE, South Africa, Nigeria and Kenya added Oct 31 2026, each with one driving test; Pakistan, region `PK`, added Nov 1 2026 with the driving licence sign test, its first exam, more to come; Spain, Portugal, Romania, Sweden, Greece, Denmark, Hungary, Belgium, Finland and Malta added Nov 2026, each with its driving theory test; Italy, region `IT`, homepage `/it` in Italian, added Nov 12 2026 with the patente B quiz, CILS B1 Cittadinanza and CELI 2 (all in Italian); `/es` is the Spain homepage in Spanish, `/pt` Portugal's in Portuguese and `/be` Belgium's in Dutch and `/gr` Greece's in Greek since Nov 2026), all rendered by `components/home/HomePage.tsx` with each other as hreflang alternates (en-US and x-default `/`, then en-GB, en-IE, en-CA, en-AU, en-NZ, en-IN and the en- tag of each newer country, `REGION_HREFLANG`), their own title, description and canonical (`REGION_HOME`, `countryHomeMetadata()`), JSON-LD in that English, and sitemap and `llms.txt` lines. Outside the US the page has that country's hero copy and second button, its driving tests and an "Also used in" row in place of Popular this week and the DMV state grid, no How it works or Reddit quotes (both DMV), its own FAQ (`faqsFor(region)`), and a catalog without US groups until the visitor searches, with a link to `/tests`. `components/CountryPills.tsx` sits above the catalog search: on a homepage each pill is a plain `<a>` to that country's page (crawlable) whose tap saves the country and does a full page load (the client router can hold a prefetch of `/` that middleware answered with a redirect under the old country), keeping the pills where they were on screen through sessionStorage; on `/tests` they are buttons and `orderGroupsForRegion()` puts the country's groups first (a group is a country's own when any member is, so the mixed "Citizenship tests" group leads in the US, UK, Canada and Australia; on a country homepage `TestCatalogGrid` `localOnly` also drops other countries' tests inside a mixed group), then worldwide (`GLOBAL`: A+, Security+, AWS, AWS SAA, AZ-900, CCNA, PMP, Gulf RN, Salesforce Admin, CFA Level I, ACCA FA, CISA, Six Sigma Green Belt and others), then other countries, then the US. `middleware.ts` (matcher `/` and `/tests`) sets the `tt-region` cookie on a first visit from Vercel's `x-vercel-ip-country` (GB maps to UK, IN to India; a country with no tests gets US) with `tt-region-src` `geo`, `default` or, after a tap, `chosen`; a saved cookie always wins, and a non-US visitor opening `/` gets a 307 to their country page. Crawlers (user-agent match) never get a location or a redirect, so `/` is indexed as the US page. A view setting only: progress is untouched. A pick (never a location guess) is also `region` + `regionAt` in `useStore`, written straight to `users/{uid}` by `setRegion` and merged on sign-in like `primaryExam` (the newer pick wins); `CountryPills` keeps the cookie in step once the account has loaded, so a pick made on another device moves this one to that country's page the first time it learns it, and a cookie-only pick goes up to the account.
- `/home-v2` (redirects to `/` since Nov 2026) — the search-first homepage, now the top of every English homepage (`components/home/SearchHome.tsx`, search in `lib/testSearch.ts`): one box over every test in every country with a flag per result (🌐 for worldwide), typo matching, DMV states ("texas permit" opens `/dmv/dashboard?state=TX`), ranked by name match, then the visitor's country, then worldwide, then this week's popularity; a tap or Enter opens the dashboard. Laid out like google.com on a phone: the site header without its logo (the page is the wordmark) and no test chip, the short footer, a large wordmark that collapses to one line while typing, a filled pill box (`.search-pill`, exempt from the global focus outline) with voice search where the browser has the Web Speech API, and under the tagline the questions answered this week (`getPopularTests().totalAnswered`, a `CountUp`), and below the box, like Google, "Trending searches" (`lib/server/trendingSearches.ts`: the searches typed at least twice in 7 days that found a test, topped up with popular test names; a tap runs the search), and above that, for anyone with progress on the device or account, "Pick up where you left off" (`hooks/useTestsInProgress.ts`: every started test with questions mastered and tests taken, the current test first; `useStartedTests` is the separate yes/no check used by Settings and the test switcher). Search drops generic words (practice, test, exam...), matches terms of 3 letters or fewer only at a word start, prefers name matches for acronyms, only offers a DMV state on a driving search (state names match as they are typed, "calif", "new y", "north car"; a bare code or alias such as "ny", "nyc" or "tx" opens that state too, after any test literally named it, like AZ-900 for "az"), keeps the last results on screen while a half-typed word matches nothing and shows "we don't have it" only once typing pauses (`SETTLE_MS`) or on Enter, and when no test has every word shows the tests with the most of them as closest matches, counted as not found (`partial`). A one-letter slip in a driving word next to a state is corrected ("california dmc"), and a search naming a state-sold family and a state, even inside a long sentence, opens that state's version ("louisiana real estate" opens `/real-estate/la/dashboard`; a state without a version offers the national test, counted as not found). Queries (sent once typing pauses, internal browsers skipped) and picks go to `/api/analytics/search` into `searchQueries/{sha1 of the query}` (count, misses, lastResults, picks, byRegion, daily) and `analytics/search`; the Searches tab on `/admin` (`components/admin/SearchesView.tsx`, `/api/admin/searches`; the old `/home-v2/searches` redirects there) is built to stay short as the collection grows: it reads only queries active in the last 90 days plus the 300 most searched ever (cached 2 minutes), groups the ways of typing one search by `searchIntentKey()` (generic words dropped, spellings folded, words sorted), counts each group over 7 days, 30 days or all time (a toggle, from `daily`), and re-runs every past miss through today's `searchAllTests()`, so a search moves from "Build these" to "Found now" by itself once its test or alias exists. Build-list rows can be marked Planned or Ignored (`searchTriage/{sha1 of the key}`, read fresh on every load) so typos and junk are handled once; plus Most searched, Latest, a 30-day chart and a filter box. Trending searches read only the last 7 days' queries (`lastAt`) for the same reason.
- `/dmv-practice-test` — the DMV landing (the homepage until Oct 2026; same copy, own canonical; the catalog's DMV `href`)
- `/dmv/dashboard` — main hub (4 tests + 4 training sets), open to guests; the state picker is a card at the top until a state is chosen (`components/dashboard/DmvStatePicker.tsx`), then one line in the hero with Change (which asks first, since switching deletes progress). State landings link here with `?state=XX`, taken only while no state is set. `/dashboard` and the old `/onboarding/select-state` redirect here (`next.config.ts`); signup no longer asks for a state
- `/test/[id]` — practice test (1-4)
- `/test/[id]/results` — test results with performance breakdown
- `/practice/[n]` — mastery-based training set n (1-4); `/training` with no set is free practice
- `/stats` — per-question performance analytics
- `/admin` — admin dashboard (restricted by email in `/lib/admin.ts`)

Guest mode allows using the app without an account; guest data converts on signup.

**DMV languages.** The DMV flow runs in English, Spanish (every state), Vietnamese (`data/viStates.ts`), Korean (`data/koStates.ts`) and Simplified Chinese (`zh`, `data/zhStates.ts`: CA, NY, NJ, MA, WA, PA, MD, VA, the states whose DMV offers the knowledge test in Chinese; Texas offers only English and Spanish and Illinois publishes no language list, so both were left out in Oct 2026, and neither Texas nor Florida, English-only since Feb 2026, offers Vietnamese). Each language has a UI dictionary (`i18n/<lang>.ts`, registered in `i18n/index.ts` `LANGUAGES`), a question bank `data/questions_<lang>.json` with the English ids, categories and option order, SEO state pages and an index under `app/<lang>/` (Chinese: `/zh/<state>-dmv-bishi-lianxi` and `/zh/gezhou-dmv-bishi-lianxi`), landing copy in `data/stateLandingData<Lang>.ts`, hreflang on every language's state and index pages, sitemap entries and a line in `lib/llmsTxt.ts`. The es, vi and ko banks mirror all 2,200 rows; the zh bank holds only the 160 universal questions plus the state-specific rows for its states and for TX and IL (translated before those were checked), and `getQuestionsZh()` in `lib/testGenerator.ts` fills every other row with English. The zh copy is machine-translated pending native review. The shuffle guard in `lib/shuffleOptions.ts` needs each language's "A and B" / "all of the above" wording; add it with a new language. The OG score card stays English for ko and zh (Inter has no CJK glyphs).

**One URL shape for every exam** (v1, state variants, v2): `/<exam>/dashboard`, `/<exam>/practice/<n>` (training set or drill, numbered from 1 in config order; v1 split sets add `?part=`, v2 drills add `?skill=id` or `?level=exam`), `/<exam>/test/<n>` and `/<exam>/test/<n>/results` (numbered from 1, never the store ID), plus `/<exam>/drill` and `/<exam>/stats` on v1. Bare `/<exam>` redirects to its dashboard. DMV is the same at the root (`/practice/<n>`, `/test/<n>`) except its dashboard, `/dmv/dashboard`. URLs come only from `getExamRoutes()` / `DMV_ROUTES` (`lib/examRoutes.ts`) and `v2Routes()` (`lib/v2/registry.ts`). Old shapes still work: `next.config.ts` maps `/training?set=n`, `/<exam>/training?set=n` and `/<exam>/train?drill=key`; `useExamTestParam` and `testIdFromParam` accept a store ID (`/cdl/test/101`) and swap the address for the numbered one; `drillFromParam` accepts a drill key or `legacyKey`. The DMV header reads "DMV Practice Test" (`common.dmvHeaderTitle`) on DMV pages and keeps the tigertest.io wordmark on the hub, settings and sign-in pages.

Signed-out visitors see `components/GuestSaveCard.tsx` (their own answer count or score, one-tap Google, email) on every dashboard and the v1 results page. In registry-exam training sets and v2 drills (not DMV) `components/GuestPromptStep.tsx` takes the place of the next question when `hooks/useGuestPrompt.ts` says so: after a correct answer that makes two in a row, once 5 are answered, then again only after 12 more (counts in sessionStorage), never on children's tests (`isChildPath()`, the `CHILD_PATH_PREFIXES` list). GA4 event `guest_prompt` records shown, skipped, google and email. **Sign-up message test** (`lib/guestPromptTest.ts`, Nov 2026, test `guestPrompt10`): English readers get a different one of ten designs (`components/guestPrompt/designs.tsx`, v01 the streak card in `components/guestPrompt/StreakCard.tsx`, which every other language gets translated) each time the step comes up, in an order shuffled once per browser (localStorage `tigertest-guest-prompt-test`; `?guestPrompt=v05` pins one for previews and counts nothing). Shown, skipped, Google and email taps, and sign-ups (an account made within an hour of a tap, credited from `trackFunnelSignup()`) go to `/api/analytics/guest-prompt` into `analytics/guestPromptTest` per design, overall, daily and for a browser's first showing (`first`); the Conversion tab's "Which sign-up message works best?" card (`components/admin/GuestPromptTestCard.tsx`, `/api/admin/guest-prompt-test`) ranks them by sign-ups per showing against v01 and previews all ten. The GA4 event carries `design` and `variant`.

People who are not Premium (guests included) and read in English get John's "tell a friend" ask, `components/SharePromptStep.tsx` (his `JohnFace` from the paywall, native share sheet or copy link; Keep practicing unlocks after 3 seconds with a fill and countdown, sharing is open at once), in place of the next question in the same three places plus the DMV `app/training` page, through `hooks/useSharePrompt.ts`: after a third correct answer in a row once they have more than 50 answers on that exam, at most once a session and once every 14 days, never again after they share (localStorage `tigertest-share-prompt`). The sign-up prompt wins when both are due, and an earned set completion comes first. GA4 event `share_prompt` (shown, skipped, share, copy), the same actions counted by `/api/analytics/share-prompt` into `analytics/sharePrompt` (total, daily, by page; internal browsers skipped), and a share also counts in `/api/analytics/share`. The shared link is `/?utm_source=friend`, so a friend's first visit is the `friend` visit source ("Friend's link" in `lib/funnel.ts`, the funnel and teach routes, Growth and Teachers tabs). The Today tab's "Tell a friend prompt" card (`buildSharePrompt()` in `lib/server/opportunities.ts`) shows shown and shared over 30 days and all time, the share rate by page, and friends' visits, signups and buyers from that source.

**Super Amazing Mode** (site-wide continuous fireworks, `components/SuperAmazingFireworks.tsx` in the root layout, flag `superAmazingMode` in localStorage) with tigers that poke their heads out from behind cards and the screen edges (`components/SuperAmazingTigers.tsx`, in the fireworks layer under the page so cards hide their bodies; a tiger peeks only where its head lands on empty background, and a card tiger ducks on scroll; off under reduced motion) is earned by turning any dashboard all green: DMV 8/8, a registry exam's sets plus tests, or every v2 step. `components/SuperAmazingCard.tsx` sits at the bottom of all three dashboards and owns the unlock: each dashboard's switch works only once that dashboard has been all green (`superAmazingUnlocks[key]`, key `dmv`, the registry exam id or `v2:<examId>`, never cleared by later progress; the fireworks stay site-wide once on). `superAmazingUnlockedAt` is only the first unlock anywhere, for the email and /admin, and never unlocks a switch (it was found on accounts that never went green). The toggle syncs to `users/{uid}.superAmazing` for /admin; logout clears the device's fireworks flag. Every dashboard must stay completable: every training set needs at least its `size` unique questions and every DMV state at least 40 state-specific questions.

**Instructor classes** (`lib/classes.ts`, free): a signed-in teacher makes a class for any test on the site at `/teach/new` (a searchable picker over `TEST_GROUPS`: the DMV with a required state, stored as examId `dmv` plus the class's `state`; a v1 registry exam or its state version; or a v2 exam, by its own id; `classExamInfo()` resolves name, home, pass mark and topic labels for all three) and posts its link, `tigertest.io/c/<code>` (5 characters, no 0/O/1/I/L). A student types the name their instructor knows them by on `/c/<code>` (no account) and lands on the exam dashboard, which shows a `ClassBanner` saying their scores are shared, with Leave. Memberships (class and quiz) live in localStorage (`tigertest-classes`); its random `memberId` is both the member doc id and the secret that lets only its holder report for that student. While signed in that list is a copy of `users/{uid}.classMemberships` (`lib/classMembershipSync.ts`, `components/ClassMembershipSync.tsx` in the root layout): joins and leaves update the account in a transaction, a guest's memberships are added to the account on sign-in, another device loads the account's list (same memberId, so it reports to the same student row), and sign-out clears the browser's copy (`tigertest-classes-uid` records which account it belongs to). `lib/classProgress.ts` queues every answer on `ExamTestPage`, `ExamTrainingPage`, the DMV `app/test/[id]` and `app/training` pages and the v2 `DrillRunner` (a finished v2 `ExamRunner` test reports every item at submit) (question, category, right or wrong) and every finished practice test, and flushes to `/api/classes/[code]/progress` every 5 seconds and on page hide. Firestore: `classes/{code}` (teacherUid, examId, memberCount) and `classes/{code}/members/{memberId}` (answered, correct, byCategory, missed and seen per question, `picks` per question (wrong answers by the option's bank index, recorded since Oct 19 2026; v1 carries the shuffle as `Question.optionOrder`, v2 reports single-choice items only), tests by number with best and attempts). The teacher's `/teach/classes/[code]` reads `/api/classes/[code]/report` (owner or site admin, `lib/server/classAuth.ts`), built by `lib/server/classReport.ts`: totals, topics weakest first, every question the class answered as the /stats `QuizRow` (most missed or lowest accuracy, tap for the options with the right answer marked and each wrong option's pick count, the class counts and the explanation; `components/teach/ClassQuestions.tsx`), and one row per student. The class page's share pieces are `components/teach/ClassShare.tsx`: Copy message for students (a ready-to-paste line with the full link, British spelling for en-GB), Copy link, and Show QR code (full screen, for the classroom board; Escape closes). An empty class shows the message and the same buttons in its No students yet card. The teacher renames a class from that page with the pencil by its name (`PATCH /api/classes/[code]`, owner or admin, which also updates `className` on the class's quizzes; students who already joined keep the old name in their local `ClassBanner` copy). A DMV class sets a new student's state on join; a student already on another state chooses to switch (resetting DMV progress, as Settings warns) or keep theirs. Before Oct 19 2026 a progress batch with no wrong answers wiped the member's `missed` and `picks` maps (an empty map in a merge write replaces the stored one); `components/exam/ClassMissRecovery.tsx` (v1 and DMV dashboards and training pages) sends each joined browser's own answer history once per class to `/api/classes/[code]/recover`, which rebuilds `missed` from answers since `joinedAt`, keeping the higher count. Wrong picks from before then cannot be recovered. Question text for the class page comes from `lib/server/classQuestions.ts`, which loads the DMV, v1 and v2 banks. `/teach` is the indexed instructor landing (`components/teach/TeachLanding.tsx`, hero animation `components/stageshow/demos/TeachClassDemo.tsx`) and the class list once a teacher has classes. It replaced the driving-school program: `/schools/*` redirects to `/teach` in `next.config.ts`, the footer links "For teachers" everywhere, and the old school pages are gone (the `/api/schools` routes and `school_accounts` data remain, unused). Not yet: Basics questions, the v1 drill page, progress from a second device for a student without an account, and teacher email digests.

**Quizzes** (`lib/quizzes.ts`, free): anyone with an account turns their own material into a Tigertest practice test, played at `tigertest.io/q/<code>` (6 characters, the class-code alphabet, so never mistaken for a class code) in three modes, Practice (the v1 mastery engine with the wrong-answer queue; `toEngineQuestion()` maps a quiz question onto `Question` with state `QUIZ` and id `<code>-q<n>`), Test (every question once, pass mark `passPct`, default 80) and Flashcards, and shared by link. Tigertest never pays for a model call to write a quiz: there is no server-side generation (the paste box that ran on our key was removed on day one). Quizzes are made through the person's connected Claude or ChatGPT: `/create` is one public page with the connector setup (Add it to Claude / ChatGPT, `#add-to-claude` and `#add-to-chatgpt`), Just ask, the FAQ and, once signed in, Your quizzes and Recently played (`CreateYourQuizzes`); `/connect` permanently redirects there. The assistant writes questions using `QUIZ_WRITING_GUIDE` from `lib/quizWritingGuide.ts` (the `get_quiz_writing_guide` tool and `write_quiz` prompt), then calls `create_quiz` to save them. There is no copied-prompt/JSON-reply workflow. Existing quizzes retain the owner editor; import helpers remain internal, not a creation workflow. Files: `lib/quizzes.ts` (types, `QUIZ_LIMITS`, validators, `quizUrl`/`quizPath`, `quizSummary`, `quizPublic`), `lib/server/quizzes.ts` (`createQuiz`, `updateQuiz` with `addQuestions`, `deleteQuiz`, `listMyQuizzes`, `listPublicQuizzes`, `getQuiz`, `setQuizFeatured`, `isPremiumUid`, `QuizError` with `.status`, `bumpCounter`, `quizAsClassDoc`, `quizReportSubject`), `lib/server/quizAuth.ts`, `lib/server/classMembers.ts` (the member-doc batch apply shared by classes and quizzes), `store/useQuizStore.ts` (localStorage `tigertest-quizzes`, mirrored to `users/{uid}.quizProgress` by `components/quiz/QuizSync.tsx` through `lib/quizSync.ts`, like the v2 store), `lib/quizProgress.ts` (join, plays) and `lib/quizClient.ts`; API routes `app/api/quizzes/` (`POST`/`GET` the account's own, `public`, `[code]` GET/PATCH/DELETE, `[code]/join`, `play`, `progress`, `report`, `report-question`); pages `app/q/[code]/{,dashboard,practice,test,test/results,flashcards,report,edit}` (the root is the share landing using the same `LandingIntro` as exam landing heroes; `/dashboard` holds personal modes/progress and named play), `/create` (setup and your quizzes), `/quizzes` (the public library), components in `components/quiz/`. Every quiz renders through these shared components, so a change to them reaches every quiz, old and new. The quiz dashboard (`QuizHome`) is laid out like the exam dashboards and shares `ProgressCard`, but its hero is its own code: when a change to the exam dashboard heroes should apply to quizzes, make it in `QuizHome` too. The footer is the short one on quiz pages (`isQuizPage` in `components/Footer.tsx`). Firestore: `quizzes/{code}` (`QuizDoc`: ownerUid, ownerName, visibility `private`/`link`/`public`/`class` (with `classCode`, `className`, `classTeacherName` while `class`), passPct, questions with stable ids `q1..qN` never reused after a delete, createdVia `web`/`import`/`mcp`, plays, memberCount, reports, nextQuestionId, `featured`), `quizzes/{code}/members/{memberId}` (the class member shape), `quizReports` (bad-question reports, also counted on the quiz), `analytics/quizzes` (counters `created_web`, `created_import`, `created_mcp`, `plays`, `questionReports`, shown as the Quizzes card on the Growth tab) and `users/{uid}.quizProgress`. Limits (`QUIZ_LIMITS`): free 3 quizzes of 50 questions, Premium 100 of 200; at least 3 questions, exactly 4 options. `publicRank` holds `plays` only while a quiz is public (deleted otherwise), so the library and sitemap order public quizzes with one single-field `orderBy`; `listPublicQuizzes` lists public quizzes with `QUIZ_PUBLIC_MIN_PLAYS` (5) plays, featured ones first and before 5 plays; the `/quizzes` library (page and `/api/quizzes/public`) passes `minPlays` 0 so every public quiz is listed, while the sitemap, `quiz_me` search and the quiz page's index tag keep the 5-play floor (`featured` is admin only, `PATCH /api/quizzes/[code]` with `{featured}`; `data/quizzes/*.json` are seed quizzes that `npx tsx scripts/seed-quiz.ts` loads for an account by email, `--featured`). A player who gives a name on the quiz page becomes a member (`joinQuiz()` in `lib/quizProgress.ts`, membership kind `quiz` in the `tigertest-classes` localStorage list), and `lib/classProgress.ts` reports their answers under examId `quiz:<code>` (`quizExamId()`) to `/api/quizzes/[code]/progress`; anonymous play only counts in `plays` (once per browser via `/api/quizzes/[code]/play`). The creator's `/q/<code>/report` is `buildClassReport()` over the quiz's members with `quizAsClassDoc()` and `quizReportSubject()` (topics are the questions' free-text `topic` labels). **Who can play** is three choices on the edit page (`QuizEditor`): Everyone (`link`, or `public` with "Also list it in the Tigertest library" ticked), Only me (`private`), and My class (`class`: one of the owner's /teach classes, checked as theirs by `classFields()` in `lib/server/quizzes.ts`; with one class it is picked for them). A class quiz is enforced on the server by `quizAccess()` in `lib/server/quizAccess.ts`: owner or admin, else a browser that sends its class membership (`CODE:memberId` from `tigertest-classes`, header `x-tigertest-classes`, added by `loadQuiz()`) for that class, checked against `classes/{code}/members/{memberId}`; `/api/quizzes/[code]` answers anyone else 403 `classOnly` with only the title and teacher name, `/q/<code>` never server-renders it, and `[code]/join` needs `classMemberId`. A non-member sees `ClassQuizGate` in `QuizLoader` (class code plus name), which posts `/api/quizzes/[code]/class-join`: it joins only the quiz's own class (via `joinClassMember()` in `lib/server/classMembers.ts`, shared with the class link) and never reveals the code. A student already in the class goes straight in and is joined to the quiz under their class name (`autoJoinClassQuiz()` in `lib/quizProgress.ts`), so the quiz report lists them. Assistants cannot play someone else's class quiz (`QUIZ_CLASS_ONLY`). MCP tools (`lib/server/mcp-tools.ts`): `get_quiz_writing_guide`, `create_quiz` and `update_quiz` (visibility `class` with `classCode`; `CLASS_REQUIRED` returns the classes to ask about, `NO_CLASSES` the /teach/new link), `list_my_classes`, `add_quiz_questions`, `list_my_quizzes`, `get_quiz` (answers only for the owner), `delete_quiz` (confirm required), `get_quiz_report`, plus the `write_quiz` prompt. Launch email: `quizzesLaunch` in `lib/email-templates.ts`, sent by `/api/cron/quizzes-launch` (13:30 UTC, key `quizzesLaunch2026`, whole consented base, accounts 7+ days old and active within a year) only while `QUIZZES_CAMPAIGN=send`; paused it logs the eligible count. SEO: `/create` and `/q/<code>` pages are crawlable, and the `quizzes` sitemap segment (`lib/sitemaps.ts`) lists `/quizzes`, `/create` and every public quiz from `listPublicQuizzes(500)` (empty without a Firestore credential), dated `QUIZZES_PAGES_UPDATED_AT` in `lib/seoDates.ts`; `lib/llmsTxt.ts` has a quizzes section. The footer Make a quiz link opens /create.

**MCP server** (`app/api/mcp/route.ts`, Streamable HTTP, stateless, 60 requests a minute per uid): Claude, ChatGPT and any MCP client sign in with OAuth 2.1 + PKCE bridged to Firebase Auth (`app/api/mcp/oauth/*` for register, authorize, callback and token; discovery at `app/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server` plus the RFC 9728 protected-resource metadata at `app/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource` (and its `/api/mcp` path-suffixed twin, both from `lib/server/mcpResourceMetadata.ts`), which the 401 from `/api/mcp` points at with a `resource_metadata` hint so clients start OAuth on their own; the consent page `app/oauth/login` can also create the account, since connecting from a chat is many people's first contact; someone already signed in is shown the client name, their account and where they will be sent, and must press Allow, never authorized by the link alone; `lib/oauthRedirect.ts` limits registered redirect URIs to https, http on loopback, or an app's own scheme, checked at register and again before a code is issued), and `lib/server/mcp-auth.ts` mints HS256 JWTs signed with `MCP_TOKEN_SECRET` and issued by `MCP_OAUTH_ISSUER_URL` (the public site URL, also used for the links tools return). Tools live in `lib/server/mcp-tools.ts` (`ToolEntry` objects pushed onto `tools`, zod input shapes, `ok()`/`fail()` from `lib/server/mcp-tool-helpers.ts`, `userId` only ever from `ctx`): the two-call quiz loop any agent can run with no state of its own (`quiz_me` takes a quiz code, `"dmv"` or search words and returns one question; `answer` grades a letter and returns the explanation, progress and the next question; `find_quiz` searches the person's quizzes and the public library; `reset_quiz_play` starts over), backed by `lib/server/mcpQuizPlay.ts` (mastery queue per account and quiz in `mcpQuizPlay/{uid}_{code}`, the same fresh-first then wrong-queue rule as the site, a play counted on the first answer, and a named member `u_<uid>` with `via: "assistant"` on someone else's quiz so the creator's report shows assistant play; `"dmv"` wraps the training-set tools on the first unmastered set), the DMV tutor tools over `lib/server/progress.ts` (state, progress, training sets, practice tests, weak areas) and the quiz authoring tools above. `/create` is the setup page people follow to add the connector, `/developers` is the page for builders and the directory submissions (endpoint, auth, tool list, example prompts, icon, privacy, `/terms`), and `MCP_SERVER_PLAN.md` and `MCP_TESTING.md` are the spec and the test notes.

**`/admin` is one page with eight tabs** (`app/admin/page.tsx`, `?tab=` in the URL, each tab loading its own API on first open through `useAdminFetch` in `components/admin/AdminTabParts.tsx`): Today (the default, below), Growth (`components/admin/GrowthTab.tsx`, `/api/admin/metrics`: KPIs, charts (new signups by day, week or month, `components/admin/PeriodBars.tsx`), the Basics card, study times, test requests, the visitor funnel), Conversion (below), Teachers, Quizzes, Searches (what people type into the test search), Questions (question quality, below) and Users (`components/admin/UsersTab.tsx`, `/api/admin/users`: the 100 most recently active accounts with delete, DMV pass rate by state, Super Amazing adoption). The old `/admin/v2`, `/admin/opportunities` and `/admin/teach` redirect to their tabs in `next.config.ts`; the header shield (`components/AdminLink.tsx`) opens `/admin`. Only the Today tab is in the page bundle; the others load with `next/dynamic` when first opened (Growth carries recharts). Every admin GET route gates with `requireAdmin()` (`lib/server/adminAuth.ts`) and serves through `cachedAdminPayload()` (`lib/server/adminCache.ts`): the last payload is kept gzipped in `adminCache/{key}` as well as in memory, so a cold serverless instance answers from one doc read; within `ttlMs` (1 to 5 minutes by route) it is a HIT, up to 24 hours it is served as STALE while a rebuild runs after the response (`after()` from `next/server`) and `useAdminFetch` polls until the fresh copy lands (the tab header says "updating"), and `?refresh=true` (the Refresh button) rebuilds before answering. The tabs are built for a phone too: a sticky tab bar, wide tables scrolling sideways with a sticky first column (`ROW` + `STICKY_COL` in `AdminTabParts.tsx`) and the Users table becoming a card list below `sm`.

The Conversion tab (`components/admin/ConversionTab.tsx`, `/api/admin/conversion`, `lib/server/conversion.ts`, maths in `lib/conversion.ts`) is laid out as questions, each card opening with a plain sentence. The server sends one packed row per account that answered, saw a paywall or paid (accounts read with a narrow field mask; only buyers' `trainingAnswerHistory` and `completedTests` are fetched, to split their activity at `subscription.purchasedAt`), every purchase as a `Sale` (day, paywall, market, US cents) and 90 days of hits per paywall; the browser does the cutting. Rule: a buyer is measured at the moment they paid, everyone else as they are now. (1) **More people are hitting paywalls. Where is the money?** The last 7, 14 or 30 days against the same number before, today left out (`comparePeriods()`): hits, purchases, revenue and purchases per 100 hits, generated sentences (which paywalls the extra hits came from, DMV against every other test, how few signed-in accounts were behind them, what the period would have sold at the old rate) and a table per paywall with signed-in people. (2) The paywall hits chart (`analytics/paywalls.daily`, moved from Growth in Nov 2026). (3) **Who pays when asked?** by market or by test (`byGroup()`): asked to pay, paid, pay rate, revenue, unpaid accounts that saw a paywall in 30 days and what they are worth at that group's rate. A market is one of Tigertest's countries, `OTHER` or `UNKNOWN`: the country of the account's last paywall view (`users/{uid}.paywallCountry`), signup (`signupOrigins`) or payment, else its picked region, else its test's country. (4) **How close is someone to paying?** the ladder by questions, tests, days active or paywall views, filtered by test and market. (5) How fast buyers buy and the typical buyer. (6) The 30 unpaid accounts closest to paying (`payChance()` from the questions x paywall views grid; cells under `MIN_CELL` 25 fall back to a broader one). The grid and the all-time paywall table are folded under More detail. `/api/analytics/paywall` also counts hits by signed-in accounts (`signedInDaily`) and by country (`byCountry`) since 2026-11-12 (`TRACKED_SINCE`), which the first card reads for the guest share. Questions use `_stats` (unique training questions plus test answers, DMV and v1 only). `tests/conversion.test.cjs` pins the rules.

The Teachers tab (`lib/server/teachAdmin.ts`, `/api/admin/teach`) tracks the channel: tiles, a teacher funnel (saw /teach, clicked Create, reached the form, made a class) and a student funnel (opened a class link, joined, answered, finished a test), the dashboard nudge (`components/TeachNudge.tsx`: browsers shown it, copied its /teach link, tapped it, overall and by exam in `nudgeExams`, counted since 2026-10-20), every class with link views against joins, practice, answers, tests, last student activity and when the teacher last opened the class page, school LMS referrals, and a 12-week table. Browser steps come from `lib/teachTracking.ts` (once per browser, internal browsers skipped) into `analytics/teach` (total and daily, landing sources and referrer hosts with dots stored as `_`) via `/api/analytics/teach`, counted since 2026-10-19 (`TEACH_TRACKING_SINCE`); class link views also land on the class doc (`joinViews`, `joinReferrers`), and the report route stamps `teacherViews` / `teacherLastViewedAt` when the owner (not an admin) opens it. Joins, practice and tests come from the classes themselves, so they cover all history. Classes made by internal accounts are hidden.

The Quizzes tab (`lib/server/quizAdmin.ts`, `/api/admin/quizzes`, `components/admin/QuizAdminView.tsx`) tracks Make a quiz: tiles, connector to quiz (accounts with an `oauth_refresh_tokens` sign-in, by assistant from `oauth_clients.clientName`, against those who made a quiz), how quizzes were made and who can play, a 12-week table, every quiz (creator and email, source, visibility, questions, plays, named players from `quizzes/{code}/members`, answers, reports, with a Feature button on public quizzes that PATCHes `featured`) and every creator. Quizzes by internal accounts are hidden; the all-time `analytics/quizzes` counters (which include them) are in the footnote.

The Basics card on the Growth tab (`components/admin/BasicsProgress.tsx`, `/api/admin/v2-progress`, `lib/server/v2Progress.ts`) covers every v2 exam with Basics (SAT, ACT, TEAS, HESI, ASVAB, ACCUPLACER). From accounts' `users/{uid}.v2` (all history, no guests): students, how many learned a skill or skipped Basics, median dashboard steps done, started and done per step in dashboard order, full tests and goals hit, exam-level drill accuracy and mastery split by whether a skill of that drill was learned, and per-skill learned counts. From every browser, guests included (`lib/v2/basicsTracking.ts` into `analytics/v2Basics` via `/api/analytics/basics`, once per browser per skill or drill, internal browsers skipped, since 2026-10-20 `BASICS_TRACKING_SINCE`): skill opened and learned, Brush up offered and tapped, exam level started and drill mastered split by Basics first or not.

The Exit questions card on the Growth tab (`components/admin/ExitQuestions.tsx`, `/api/admin/exit-questions`, logic in `lib/exitQuestions.ts`, built nightly by `/api/cron/aggregate-stats` into `globalStats/exitQuestions`) finds questions that may drive people away: for every account quiet 14+ days, the exam holding its last answer gets the exit, classed as bounce (under 3 answers), passed (passed a practice test), test (last act was finishing a test) or question (left mid-practice); question exits are ranked per exam by exits above expected (people who saw it times the exam's exit rate per question seen), with lift, share after a miss, miss rate, sitting length and miss streak, and the question text, options, key and explanation on tap. Accounts and timestamped answers only, so no guests and no v2 exams.

The Dashboard layout card on the Growth tab (`components/admin/LayoutUsage.tsx`, `/api/admin/layout-usage`, `lib/server/layoutUsage.ts`) asks whether v1 exams laid out Set N, Test N (the 27 switched on Oct 22 2026 and later `testSets()` exams) get more use than sets-first ones: share of people who started and finished a practice test and median answers, all time and for new users in a window before and after the switch (first 7 days each, sets-first exams as the control, only exams with new users in both windows), plus per exam. Accounts only, internal skipped. Reading every user doc takes longer than one serverless call, so it scans in resumable chunks like the questions backfill: the card POSTs one 35-second chunk at a time (running sums and cursor in `analytics/layoutUsageRun`, with a lease), the last chunk saves the result to `globalStats/layoutUsage`, and GET reads that; the card rebuilds when the result is over 12 hours old or on Rebuild.

The visitor funnel on the Growth tab (`lib/funnel.ts`, `/api/analytics/funnel`) counts signed-out browsers once per step: first visit, first question answered, signup page opened, account created, plus the DMV state picker (`dmv_picker` shown, `dmv_state` chosen there, since 2026-10-29; a `?state=` link skips the picker and counts in neither), shown as the "Never chose a DMV state" tile. The funnel route also counts each first visit by the visitor's Tigertest country from Vercel's `x-vercel-ip-country` (`visit_region_<US|CA|UK|IE|AU|NZ|IN|OTHER>`, since 2026-10-31) and stamps the ISO `country` on `signupOrigins/{uid}`. GA4 gets `state_selected` with `state` and `via` (`picker` or `link`) for everyone. Counts go to `analytics/funnel`; each new account's origin (`tried` first or `cold`, plus its first visit's `source` and `landing` path, kept in localStorage from the visit step) goes to `signupOrigins/{uid}`, kept out of `users/{uid}` so it never races the first load.

The Today tab (`lib/server/opportunities.ts`, `/api/admin/opportunities`, view in `components/admin/OpportunitiesView.tsx`) is the what-to-do-next page: a By country card (US, Canada, UK, Ireland, Australia, New Zealand, India, then worldwide exams: exams in use, answers, learners, visitors, signups and revenue; practice filed by `regionOfTest()`, visitors and signups by IP country; tapping a row filters the exam table), every exam's practice (with a country filter, a search box and an all-time column; the per-exam table that used to sit on Growth), paywall views, purchases and revenue (what free US exams would earn at the DMV's revenue per answer feeds the free-exam note only, not a column; every exam's stats page sells Premium, in every country, at the visitor's local price), paywalls with purchases per 100 views, visitors and signups by source, weekly retention cohorts, a data-health list and generated insights. It reads three Stripe listings read-only (completed checkouts for older payments' `location`, refunds, the webhook's events). The Stripe webhook now stores `location` and `exam` on each `payments` doc (`lib/purchaseAttribution.ts`; the DMV dashboard sends `training_set_4` / `practice_test_4` for the third set and test too) and marks `charge.refunded` as `refunded` / `partially_refunded` with `refundedCents`, never revoking Premium. **English paywall test** (`lib/paywallTest.ts`, ids in `lib/paywallTestIds.ts`, test `paywallJohn1`): `PaywallModal` splits English readers 50/50 by browser (localStorage `tigertest-paywall-test`, `?paywall=john|classic` pins it) between John's chip-in `SupportPaywallModal` and the classic Premium paywall; other languages always get the classic one. Each open counts in `analytics/paywallTest` (views and distinct browsers per variant, via `/api/analytics/paywall-test`) and stamps `users/{uid}.paywallTest`; every checkout call sends `paywallVariant`, which rides the Stripe metadata onto the `payments` doc. The Today tab shows purchases per 100 browsers per side with a two-proportion p-value. **Return messages** (Nov 13 2026): on both sides, half of browsers (`ret: "new"`, assigned once) get a different message the second and third time they hit a paywall and half (`"same"`) keep the first one as the comparison (`notePaywallHit()`, visits counted in the same localStorage record, a reopen within 10 minutes is the same visit, `?paywallHit=2|3` previews without counting; copy in `RETURN_HEADLINES` in `PaywallModal` and `MESSAGES` in `SupportPaywallModal`, English only). The visit and arm ride every view (`analytics/paywallTest.hits.{1,2,3}.{views,new,same}`) and checkout (`paywallHit` and `paywallReturn` on the `payments` doc), and the card lists views and purchases per visit and arm with a p-value for new against first again. At the top is a daily update written by Claude (`lib/server/dailyBrief.ts`, `claude-opus-5-5`): the cron `/api/cron/daily-brief` (weekdays 10:00 UTC, so by 7am Eastern in summer) sends the page's numbers, the teachers and schools numbers from `buildTeachAdmin()` (classes and school links new since the previous update, so a first class or first school leads the headline), plus the per-country numbers and the previous update's to the API and saves a headline and 3 to 5 bullets to `dailyBriefs/{Eastern date}`; alongside, a second call with the web search tool (`lib/server/webMentions.ts`) looks for pages talking about Tigertest since the search homepage launched (`MENTIONS_SINCE` in `lib/webMentionsSince.ts`, Nov 1 2026), keeps up to 15 links across updates in `mentions` (new ones, not in the previous day's update, are marked and fed to the brief as `newWebMentions`) and shows them under the bullets (both routes allow 300 seconds); "Write a fresh one" on the page calls `/api/admin/daily-brief`. Without `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` the cron skips and the page says so; the six rule-based notes are folded under it.

**School referrals** (`lib/schoolReferral.ts`, `lib/schoolHosts.ts`, `/api/analytics/school-referral`): a visit whose referrer is a school LMS (Canvas, Blackboard, Brightspace, Moodle, Schoology, Google Classroom, Sakai) or a .edu / k12 site is reported once per browser per school and course, signed in or not (internal browsers skipped). Each host is a doc in `schoolReferrals` (visits, daily, landings with '/' stored as '|', and `courses` keyed by the course id read from the referrer path). The first visit from a new host, or a new course at a known host, emails John (`CAMPAIGN_REPLY_TO`, transactional, capped at 20 a day in `analytics/schoolReferralAlerts`): each is a teacher who posted a Tigertest link.

The "Questions by exam" table's Enrolled column counts distinct people with at least one answer per exam: one doc per (exam, person) in `enrollments` (`lib/enrollment.ts`, `/api/analytics/enrollment`), keyed `{exam}__u_{uid}` or `{exam}__b_{browserId}` for guests; signing in claims the browser's guest docs so a converted guest counts once. Accounts that predate it are seeded by `/api/admin/backfill-questions` (`enrollmentsBackfilledAt`).

`/api/admin/backfill-questions` is resumable: each call scans users for about 35s, saves its cursor and running sums in `analytics/questionsBackfillRun` (with a lease so two tabs never scan twice) and returns `done: false`; the admin pages loop through `lib/questionsBackfillClient.ts` until `done: true`. The anonymous answer counter (`/api/analytics/questions`) is capped per IP at 2,000 answers an hour and 6,000 a day (`lib/server/answerRateLimit.ts`, hashed IPs in `analyticsRateLimits`, TTL field `expiresAt`); dropped answers are tallied in `analytics/questions.rateLimited`.

**Internal accounts never count in /admin.** `lib/internal.ts` (`isInternalEmail`: `ADMIN_EMAILS`, `@johnbrophy.net`, `@stensul.com`) is applied on read by `/api/admin/metrics`, `/api/admin/users` and `/api/admin/backfill-questions` (uids from one cached Auth listing in `lib/server/internalUsers.ts`: users, payments, test requests and signup origins are dropped), and on write: an internal login marks the browser (`tigertest-internal` in localStorage, set in `AuthContext`, never cleared), after which the anonymous counters (questions answered, funnel steps, paywall hits, shares) are not sent from it even signed out. Test browsers never send them either (`isTestBrowser()`: any host but tigertest.io, so localhost and preview deployments, which write to the live Firestore; Claude's own browser, user agent `Claude/`; and `navigator.webdriver` automation), and quiz plays follow the same rule; the paywall and funnel-signup routes also drop a request whose token is internal. Counts recorded before the flag existed stay in the aggregates. **Bots never count either** (Nov 2026): every `/api/analytics/*` route, quiz plays and email clicks drop a request whose user agent `isBotUserAgent()` (`lib/botUserAgent.ts`, also the middleware's crawler list) flags: named crawlers, self-named automation (HeadlessChrome, curl...) and desktop Chrome 5 or more majors behind current stable (scraper fleets in China, Hong Kong and Singapore ran Chrome 103 to 150 and filled the By country card with fake visitors). Phones, ChromeOS and Windows 7/8 are never judged by version. Bump `CHROME_ANCHOR_*` if Chrome's cadence changes. The stats cron has its own, broader `isTest()`.

### Additional Exams (CDL, HTL, CST, CRCST)

Non-DMV exams reuse the DMV components and store, namespaced by ID range and a
pseudo state code so progress never collides with DMV data. All of them are driven by the registry in `lib/exams.ts` (`ExamConfig`: state code, ID bases, slug, landing path, blueprint, training sets, category labels, dashboard copy). Each exam's bank is its own module, `lib/banks/<examId>.ts` (English plus any translated banks), registered into `lib/examData.ts` by `registerExamBanks()` and imported for that side effect only by the exam's stats and drill pages and loaded on demand by the shared test and practice pages, so a page bundles its own exam's questions and no other's (and never the DMV banks: answer shuffling lives in `lib/shuffleOptions.ts`); `lib/banks/all.ts` loads every bank for scripts only; `lib/examTestGenerator.ts` builds blueprint-weighted deterministic tests (test N takes slice N of each category pool, sorted by question ID) and training sets (one or more categories per set, or a test's own questions; see DMV-style test sets below). No training session runs much past 50 questions: a set over 60 (`TRAINING_PART_MAX`) shows on the dashboard as parts of about 50, each its own step (`?set=N&part=P`; without `part` the page opens the first unfinished one). A part is a contiguous run of the set in question-ID order, and its start IDs and sizes are generated into `lib/trainingParts.json` by `npx tsx scripts/build-training-parts.ts` (the dashboard does not bundle banks); progress stays stored per set id and parts only filter it, so server progress, admin and emails are unchanged and existing mastery fills part 1 first. The script cuts where the category changes whenever sizes allow (a single-category set is cut evenly), and every split set carries hand-written `partNames` (what each part actually covers, e.g. CDL "Night Driving", "Weather Driving", "Mountains & Railroad Crossings"; Spanish ones in the CDL translation), shown on the dashboard, the training page and its "Next:" button; without them a part falls back to "<set>, part 2 of 3", and the script warns when the count does not match. Re-read the stems and rename when a split changes. Re-run the script (`--check` reports staleness and any set `size` that does not match its bank) after changing a bank or a set's categories. Pages under `app/<slug>/` are thin wrappers around `components/exam/*` (dashboard, drill, stats) plus a static SEO landing; the test, results and practice pages of every v1 and v2 exam are one shared set of routes, `app/[slug]/test/[id]`, `app/[slug]/test/[id]/results` and `app/[slug]/practice/[n]` (`components/exam/SharedExamAppPage.tsx`, layout `SharedExamAppLayout.tsx`), which find the exam by its `slug` (`examForAppSlug()` in `lib/examAppRoute.ts`) and import its bank on demand (`loadExamBank()`, one chunk per bank, so a bank file must be named by the exam id). They replaced about 650 per-exam copies that broke production deploys on Vercel's 2,048-route limit in Nov 2026: never add `test/[id]`, `test/[id]/results` or `practice/[n]` under a new exam's folder (a per-exam folder would win over the shared route and add about ten routes back). State variants keep their own `[state]` routes (`app/<slug>/page.tsx`, or `landingPath` when it lives elsewhere). `lib/testCatalog.ts` lists every test (DMV + registry) for the `/tests` hub, header switcher and footer.
**Training sets are named by topic (Nov 2026).** Every registry exam's training sets are content-area sets with a topic name ("Safety & Compliance", never "Training Set 1"): one or more categories per set, 35 to 60 questions where the pools allow (a bigger single topic splits into parts with hand-written `partNames`), usually four sets, in the official outline's order with a small set last (the last training step is Premium), names of about 35 characters or fewer in the outline's own words, and `weightLabel` "13 of 50 test questions". From Oct 22 to Nov 11 2026, 84 exams instead trained DMV-style, set N holding exactly practice test N's questions (`testSets()` in `lib/exams.ts`, `test` on the set def, named "Training Set N"); John chose topic names over that pairing, accepting that an early test now includes topics not trained yet. The machinery (`testSets`, `hasTestSets`, `TEST_SET_NAMES`) is still there but no exam uses it: do not use it for a new exam. Those 84 kept their set IDs, so `lib/testSetMigration.ts` (`BACK_TO_TOPIC_SETS`, the list of them) regroups stored progress by question on the dashboard and training page through `useTestSetMigration`, loading the exam's bank on demand, and drops set IDs the exam no longer has; it is a no-op once regrouped. The Dashboard layout card on /admin keeps those 84 as its "interleaved" group. Translated exams carry set names in `translations[lang].trainingSets`. Every registry dashboard still alternates training and tests (since Nov 2026, so people test themselves early): `ExamDashboard` spreads the tests evenly through the training steps, test i after step ceil((i+1)*P/T), so a test always comes last (CDL: two or three sets, then a test); only the on-screen order changes, progress is stored by set and test ID, and the section headings (`copy.trainingHeading` / `testsHeading`) are no longer shown.
- **CDL** (general knowledge, `/cdl/*`, landing `/cdl-practice-test`): `data/cdl-questions.json` (600 questions, 13 categories), test IDs 101-106, training set IDs 121-126 (`setIdBase` 120, so old sequential-set progress at 101-112 is ignored rather than misread), state `CDL`, 80% pass. 9 safe driving, 7 inspection, 5 basic control, 5 braking, 4 systems, 4 cargo, 4 emergencies, 3 hazards, 3 alcohol/drugs, 2 weather, 2 railroad, 1 night, 1 mountain per test. Also in Spanish: `data/cdl-questions_es.json` (same ids, categories and keys; questions in "usted", UI copy in "tú" like the DMV), the `translations.es` block on its config, and the footer `LanguageSwitcher` (English and Español only) on `/cdl/*` app pages, never on a landing. Spanish landing `/es/examen-practica-cdl` (`translations.es.landingPath`, so `getExamByPath` gives it the CDL header and theme, and the exams sitemap lists it; hreflang both ways with the English landing): both landings carry the DMV homepage `LanguageSwitcher` in the site header (English and Español, showing the page's own language via `current`, and navigating to the other landing), and the Spanish one's `ExamLandingHero`/`ExamLandingCTA` take `lang="es"`, which switches the app to Spanish on the way in; screenshots `public/landing/cdl-{mobile,desktop}-es.png`.

**International driving tests** (Oct 2026, 15 exams: UK, Canada and Australia, five each, hub groups "UK driving and instructor theory tests", "Canada driving tests", "Australia learner driver tests"). Each is a plain registry exam built like the DMV: 200 original questions from the jurisdiction's official handbook in its own rules, spelling and units, four 50-question tests with DMV-style test sets (Set N = Test N), free. Signs are described in words (no images). Hazard perception video tests, UK and Australia, are not modelled and every landing says so. No jurisdiction publishes topic weights, so the blueprints follow the handbooks' chapters. Question sources (Python, one file per category), each `build.py` and a `sources.md` of official URLs are in `tools/intl/<id>/` with `spec.json` (the wiring input); edit the sources and rebuild rather than the JSON (build scripts were written in a scratch folder, so check their input paths first). Brand colours: UK navy (hue 222), Canada red (356), Australia green (150). Mascots are borrowed from the matching US exam through `BORROWED_SETS` in `lib/tigerAssets.ts` (DMV tiger for car tests and ADI, the motorcycle tiger for UK motorcycle, the CDL tiger for LGV and PCV), so catalog cards, the switcher and landings show a tiger; drop an entry once that exam gets its own set.
- **UK car theory test** (`/uk-theory/*`, landing `/uk-driving-theory-test`): `data/uk-car-theory-questions.json` (200 questions: alertness & attitude 20, safety & your vehicle 24, safety margins & vehicle handling 28, hazards & vulnerable road users 32, motorway rules 20, rules of the road 32, road & traffic signs 24, documents, incidents & emergencies 20), test IDs 6701-6704, sets 6701-6704 (by topic), state `UKCAR`, 86% pass. 5/6/7/8/5/8/6/5 per test.

**Teacher-led international exams** (Oct 2026, 10 exams set as class practice by teachers and trainers, all in `TEACHER_LED` and `NON_DRIVING_TESTS`): v1 SIA door supervisor, 18th Edition, CFSC, Red Seal electrician and White Card below; v2 KS2 SATs, GCSE Maths, OSSLT, NAPLAN Year 9 and NSW Selective in the v2 section. Hub groups "UK school tests" (timestables, ks2, gcsemaths), "UK workplace and trade certificates" (uksia, uk18ed), "Canada school and trade tests" (osslt, capal, caelec) and "Australia school and work certificates" (naplan, nswsel, auwhite) sit right after each country's driving group, each with `RELATED_OVERRIDES`. Theme hues: uksia 128 green, uk18ed 50 gold, capal 110 forest, caelec 60 brass, auwhite 28 orange; ks2 280, gcsemaths 82, osslt 232, naplan 142, nswsel 250. Mascots are borrowed through `BORROWED_SETS` (uksia security, uk18ed/caelec/auwhite OSHA, capal hunter, ks2 times tables).

- **Unvented G3** (`ukg3`, the knowledge assessment of the unvented hot water storage systems certificate that UK plumbers and heating engineers hold to install, commission and maintain unvented cylinders under Building Regulations Part G3, e.g. BPEC Domestic Hot Water Storage Systems (Unvented), LCL Awards Hot Water Systems and Safety, `/unvented-hot-water/*`, landing `/unvented-hot-water-g3-practice-test`): `data/uk-unvented-g3-questions.json` (200 original questions from Building Regulations 2010 Schedule 1 G3, Approved Document G Section G3 (2015 edition with 2016 and 2024 amendments; the 2024 changes touch G4 only), Water Supply (Water Fittings) Regulations 1999 Schedule 2 paragraphs 17 to 23, HSE HSG274 Part 2 and manufacturer commissioning and servicing instructions: Building Regulations and Part G3 24, system types and principles 20, inlet controls and expansion 28, thermostats, cut-outs and relief valves 32, tundish and discharge pipework 32 (D1, D2, Table 3.1 sizing sums, terminations), temperature control and legionella 20, installation and controls 16, commissioning and handover 12, maintenance and fault finding 16), test IDs 26801-26804, sets 26801-26804 (by topic: Regulations & System Types 44, Inlet Controls & Safety Devices 60, Discharge Pipework & Temperature 52, Install, Commission & Maintain 44), state `UKG3`, prefix `UKG3-`, 70% readiness line (Tigertest's: no awarding body publishes a pass mark). 6/5/7/8/8/5/4/3/4 per test, Tigertest's split along BPEC's four modules and AD G's order (no weights published). The real certificate (BPEC) is three assessments, one practical, all to pass; providers describe a multiple-choice paper of about 80 minutes, a short written paper and a practical job sheet; certificate valid 5 years; written and practical parts not modelled. England's AD G only; Wales (its own AD G) and Scotland (Building Standards) differ and are not asked. No fees, card terms or fixed manufacturer settings (pre-charge, T&P set points). Kept clear of `ukwater` (only the hot water paragraphs of the Water Fittings Regulations used), `uk18ed`, `cscs` and `caplumb`. Built by `tools/next100/ukg3/build.py`; sources in `tools/next100/ukg3/sources.md`. The real tests: each C&G 6035-02 unit is an e-volve online multiple-choice test (mostly 40 questions in 75 minutes, H&S 42), graded pass, merit or distinction; the ST0303 EPA knowledge test paper 1 is 60 four-option questions in 120 minutes (distinction 50), with option papers for gas (ACS CCN1 and CENWAT), heat pumps and solar thermal (30 in 60 minutes each, 18 to pass). England figures: Approved Documents H (2015), G (2015 with 2016 amendments) and L Volume 1 (2021 with 2023 amendments; the figures used also hold in the 2026 edition, in force from 24 March 2027), HSE legionella, ladder and asbestos guidance, RIDDOR reg 4. Water Regulations, unvented storage, gas and BS 7671 only lightly touched (siblings ukwater, ukg3, ukgas, uk18ed); no joist notch figures (no longer in Approved Document A). LPG 37 and 28 mbar) 16, unsafe situations (IGEM/G/11 Ed 2 with 2022 and 2024 amendments: ID and AR, turn-off order, DANGER DO NOT USE, warning notices, RIDDOR 11(2) within 14 days) 20, gas safety devices and controls 16, chimneys and flues (reg 27, 32, L56 Appendix 3 flue flow and spillage tests, AD J Table 5) 20, appliance installation (regs 26, 28 to 30, 33, gas rate and heat input sums, combustion analysis) 20), test IDs 27001-27004 (50 questions each), sets 27001-27005 (by topic: legislation and emergency actions 40, combustion, CO and ventilation 48, chimneys and appliances 40, tightness testing and meters 36, unsafe situations and safety devices 36), state `UKGAS`, prefix `UKGAS-`, 70% readiness line (Tigertest's: certification bodies run ACS as practical plus theory assessments and publish no question count, time or pass mark). 6/4/4/4/4/5/4/5/4/5/5 per test, Tigertest's split over the criteria's sections (no official weights). The practical assessment (pipework, live tightness testing, combustion analysis, appliance work) is not modelled. Great Britain law (GSIUR extends to England, Wales and Scotland; Northern Ireland has its own regulations); Building Regulations items are England's Approved Document J. No fees, CPD, registration costs or paid-standard figures (IGEM/UP/1B permitted drops, purge volumes, BS 5440 vent sizes per kW, terminal distances); the 21 mbar meter outlet figure was dropped because Issue 20.1 removed it. **Second teacher-led batch** (Oct 2026, 30 more exams wired the same way). UK: v1 CSCS, Level 2 Food Hygiene, Personal Licence, First Aid at Work and Care Certificate below; v2 GCSE Maths Higher, GCSE Combined Science, GCSE Computer Science, 11+ and Functional Skills Maths in the v2 section. "UK school tests" is now timestables, ks2, elevenplus, gcsemaths, gcsehigher, gcsescience, gcsecs, fsmaths and "UK workplace and trade certificates" cscs, uk18ed, uksia, ukaplh, ukfood, ukfaw, ukcare, both big enough that `RELATED_OVERRIDES` no longer pads them. The hue wheel is crowded, so a theme within 8 degrees of another differs by at least 15 points of saturation or 8 of lightness: gcsehigher 286 violet, gcsescience 103 leaf green, gcsecs 180 at 35% saturation, elevenplus 245 indigo, fsmaths 15 rust, cscs 46 khaki, ukfood 305 plum, ukaplh 335 wine, ukfaw 124 green, ukcare 315 mauve. Borrowed mascots: elevenplus times tables, fsmaths GED, cscs OSHA, ukfood food handler, ukaplh alcohol, ukfaw CPR, ukcare CNA. Canada: v1 Ontario PSW, Standard First Aid, WHMIS, Ontario Security Guard and Red Seal Automotive, Plumber, Carpenter and Welder below; v2 REx-PN and EQAO Grade 9 Math in the v2 section. "Canada school and trade tests" is split into "Canada school tests" (osslt, eqao9), "Canada healthcare and first aid" (rexpn, capsw, casfa; padded by `RELATED_OVERRIDES` with nclexpn, cna, cpr and their UK twins) and "Canada trades and workplace certificates" (caelec, caplumb, cacarp, caweld, caauto, cawhmis, caguard, capal). Hues: rexpn 276 deep purple, eqao9 255 indigo, capsw 325 plum, casfa 2 crimson, cawhmis 37 amber, caguard 228 navy, caauto 205 slate at 22% saturation, caplumb 212 water blue, cacarp 24 wood brown, caweld 16 spark orange. Borrowed mascots: rexpn NCLEX-PN, capsw CNA, casfa CPR, caguard security, cawhmis and the Red Seal trades OSHA. Australia: v1 RSA, RCG, Food Safety Supervisor, First Aid (HLTAID011), Forklift Licence and Working at Heights below; v2 NAPLAN Year 7, NSW OC test, Victorian Selective and HSC Maths Standard 2 in the v2 section. "Australia school and work certificates" is split into "Australia school tests" (ocnsw, nswsel, naplan7, naplan, vicsel, hscmaths, youngest first) and "Australia work certificates" (auwhite, auwah, auforklift, aufirstaid, aufss, aursa, aurcg). The orange, amber and green parts of the wheel were full, so the builders' hues moved: naplan7 240 royal blue, ocnsw 325 raspberry, vicsel 256 violet blue, hscmaths 168 eucalyptus at 38% saturation, aursa 352 burgundy, aurcg 269 deep purple, aufss 95 leaf green, aufirstaid 3 muted red at 45% saturation, auforklift 21 burnt orange, auwah 208 sky blue. Borrowed mascots: aursa and aurcg alcohol server, aufss food handler, aufirstaid CPR, auforklift forklift, auwah OSHA. auwah is the first five-digit `idBase` (10000, tests and sets 10001-10004 (by topic)); `getExamForTestId` and `getExamForSetId` are plain range checks, so nothing assumes four digits.

Real test (UHR under Förordning (2026:451), checked November 2026): in Swedish, on paper, four options and one right; at the first sitting (15 August 2026, a trial) 60 questions in 90 minutes, 40 scored, 28 of 40 to pass, per press reports; the length of the April to May 2027 sitting is not yet announced, and a Swedish-language test follows later.
